## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-12T18:25:29.089Z

```
Read-only research in ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/ (TypeScript Terraria remake).

The buff system lives in ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/stats/Buffs.ts (BuffType enum + BuffState). Enemy debuffs live in ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/entities/Enemy.ts.

Task: map EVERY place buffs/debuffs are GRANTED or CONSUMED in the implementation, so I can audit against vanilla. Answer these questions with file:line evidence (read the code, don't guess):

1. PLAYER-SIDE DEBUFF SOURCES: search all of game/src for code that applies a debuff TO THE PLAYER. Check: NPC contact damage (Enemy.ts / Player.ts hurt handling) — does an enemy hit ever apply Poisoned/Venom/OnFire/Cursed Inferno/Ichor/Frostburn/Darkness/Silenced/Slow/Weak/Confused/Broken Armor/Suffocation/Chilled/Frozen/Stunned/Electrified? grep for things like `apply(`, `buffs.apply`, `OnFire`, `Poisoned`, `addBuff` across Player.ts, Enemy.ts, Projectile files, stats/TouchDamage.ts. List which player debuffs can actually be RECEIVED in practice vs which BuffTypes are dead code.

2. PLAYER buffImmune / IMMUNITY: is there ANY debuff immunity system (accessories like Bezoar/Ankh Shield/Adhesive Bandage/Medicated Bandage, Obsidian Skin granting fire immunity, armor immunity)? grep `buffImmune`, `immune`, `fireImmune`, `lavaImmune`. Report whether accessories grant debuff immunity anywhere (check items/accessories data files).

3. WEAPON→ENEMY DEBUFF APPLICATION: how do weapons/projectiles inflict debuffs on enemies? Find the melee enchant (Imbue) chain, flail/statusNPC chain, projectile debuff application (e.g. Frostburn arrows, Ichor bullets, Venom). List which enemy debuffs are actually inflictable: poison/venom/cursed/ichor/confused/onFire/frostburn/midas/betsys/stoned/electrified/omensight?

4. NPC SIDE buffImmune: in Enemy.ts, is there an NPC buff-immunity table (vanilla NPC.buffImmune), e.g. undead immune to poison/venom/bleeding, fire immune enemies? Check what happens when an inflict is attempted on an immune NPC.

5. AddBuff merge semantics: read stats/Buffs.ts BuffState.apply — confirm it takes max (no shortening). Also find who calls apply with what durations for the campfire/lantern/honey loops (Game.ts or Player.ts scan loops).

6. Where are BuffState getters consumed in the stat aggregation (meleeDamage, crit, defense, minion count, spawn rate)? Point to the aggregation file(s) — likely a Player.ts stats function. List which BuffType effects actually reach game math vs are declared-but-unused (e.g. Sharpened armor pen +12, Bewitched/WarTable minion slots, Battle/Calming spawn rate, Star in Bottle mana regen, Lucky, MoonLeech, Inferno aura, Dangersense, Hunter, Spelunker, BiomeSight, Gravitation, Invisibility, Warmth, Flipper, Water Walking, Night Owl, Shine lighting).

Report each answer with absolute file:line references. Be very thorough. Do not modify anything.
```

213	/** Main.buffNoSave 全名单（Main.cs:8896-8990：不写入玩家存档的 buff;
214	 *  含 173-181 整段 for 循环）——本仓仅存 BUFF_DEFS 已映射项,交集生效 */
215	const BUFF_NO_SAVE = new Set([
216	  20, 22, 23, 24, 28, 29, 30, 31, 34, 35, 37, 38, 39, 43, 44, 46, 47, 48,
217	  58, 59, 60, 62, 63, 64, 67, 68, 69, 70, 72, 80, 87, 88, 89, 93, 94, 95,
218	  96, 97, 98, 99, 100, 103, 119, 120, 125, 126, 133, 134, 135, 137, 139,
219	  140, 144, 146, 147, 150, 158, 159, 161, 163, 164, 170, 171, 172, 173, 174,
220	  175, 176, 177, 178, 179, 180, 181, 182, 187, 188, 194, 195, 196, 197, 198,
221	  199, 205, 213, 214, 215, 263, 271, 320, 321, 322, 325, 335, 348, 353, 355,
222	  366, 385, 386,
223	]);
224	/** vanilla buff id → 本仓 BuffType 反查（存档恢复用） */
225	const BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA = new Map<number, BuffType>(
226	  (Object.keys(BUFF_DEFS) as unknown as BuffType[]).map((t) => [BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff, t]),
227	);
228	
229	/** Buff 名:原版 BuffName.<Internal>(Lang 未就绪回退中文兜底) */
230	export function buffName(t: BuffType): string {
231	  return Lang.buffName(BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff) ?? BUFF_DEFS[t].fallbackName;
232	}
233	
234	/** Buff 描述:自有键(机制数值是本仓库实现,不用原版通用描述) */
235	export function buffDesc(t: BuffType): string {
236	  return Lang.text(BUFF_DEFS[t].descKey);
237	}
238	
239	/** HP/s 汇总表（UpdateBuffs lifeRegen 项换算：lifeRegen N = N/2 HP/s） */
240	const HP_PER_SEC: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
241	  [BuffType.Regen]: 2,   // lifeRegen+4
242	  [BuffType.Honey]: 1,   // lifeRegen+2
243	};
244	
245	/** DoT 分块大小（Player.cs:19136-19204 结算 + HurtLifeRegen:19207）：burned/
246	 *  suffocating 走 -600 阈值分支 → 每 600 life-regen 点弹 HurtLifeRegen(5)；
247	 *  其余（OnFire 等）走通用 -120 分支 → 每 120 点弹 1。换算到时间：
248	 *  Burning(−60/t)=每 10t 弹 5（≈3 次/秒飘字）、Suffocation(−40/t)=每 15t 弹 5、
249	 *  OnFire(−8/t)=每 15t 弹 1。掉血须整块弹出并伴随 LifeRegen 色(255,60,70)
250	 *  dot 飘字（CombatText.cs:24 / NewText dot:true），非每帧静默 1 HP 滴流 */
251	const DOT_CHUNK: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
252	  [BuffType.Burning]: 5,
253	  [BuffType.Suffocation]: 5,
254	};
255	
256	/** 环境 DoT 表（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911 的 onFire→burned→suffocating 顺序，
257	 *  速率 = lifeRegen 负项 / 2 = HP/s）。每个 DoT 结算前先清零正 lifeRegen——
258	 *  恢复类 buff 不能抵消 DoT（tick 内消费） */
259	const ENV_DOTS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [BuffType, number]> = [
260	  [BuffType.OnFire, -4],      // lifeRegen-8（:18793，vampireSeed 分支未移植）
261	  [BuffType.Burning, -30],    // lifeRegen-60（:18862，另 moveSpeed×0.5 在 moveSpeedMult）
262	  [BuffType.Suffocation, -20], // lifeRegen-40（:18889）
263	];
264	
265	export class BuffState {
266	  active = new Map<BuffType, number>(); // 剩余秒（≈buffTime/60）
267	  campfireAccum = 0;
268	  /** 狼人回复独立累加器（:11496 lifeRegen++ 恒 0.5 HP/s，与篝火强度/节拍无关） */
269	  werewolfAccum = 0;
270	  lanternAccum = 0;
271	  /** 当前篝火光环强度(Game 每 20tick 扫描写入:篝火+1,心灯额外+1) */
272	  campfireHeal = 1;
273	  /** 心灯(lifeRegen+2 = 1 HP/s,Player.cs:12247):Game 扫描写入 0/1 */
274	  lanternHeal = 0;
275	  /** AddBuff 语义（Player.cs:5138-5150）：同类取 max 不缩短——浸入类 buff 可每帧无脑续期 */
276	  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
277	    const cur = this.active.get(type);
278	    if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
279	  }
280	  has(type: BuffType): boolean {
281	    return this.active.has(type);
282	  }
283	  remove(type: BuffType) {
284	    this.active.delete(type);
285	  }
286	  /** 直写剩余秒（原版 `buffTime[i] = n` 赋值语义，可缩短——区别于 apply 的
287	   *  AddBuff max 合并）。消费方：玩家微光穿落 buffTime=6t 刷新（Player.cs:11416） */
288	  set(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
289	    this.active.set(type, durationSec);
290	  }
291	  /** 存档序列化（Player.cs:53913-53925 buffType+buffTime 槽位语义）:
292	   *  存 vanilla id + 剩余秒;Main.buffNoSave 名单(Main.cs:8896-8990)过滤不落盘的 buff */
293	  toSave(): Array<[number, number]> {
294	    const out: Array<[number, number]> = [];
295	    for (const [t, sec] of this.active) {
296	      const v = BUFF_DEFS[t]?.vanillaBuff;
297	      if (v === undefined || BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
298	      out.push([v, Math.max(1, Math.round(sec))]);
299	    }
300	    return out;
301	  }
302	  /** 读档恢复（旧档/导入缺省=无 buff;未知 vanilla id 静默丢弃——后续接入的 buff 不丢档） */
303	  applySaved(list: Array<[number, number]> | undefined): void {
304	    if (!list) return;
305	    for (const [v, sec] of list) {
306	      if (BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
307	      const t = BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA.get(v);
308	      if (t !== undefined) this.active.set(t, sec);
309	    }
310	  }
311	  /** 本帧弹出的 DoT 分块伤害合计（HurtLifeRegen 语义,Player 消费后弹出飘字并清零） */
312	  private dotChunkOut = 0;
313	  consumeDotChunk(): number {
314	    const c = this.dotChunkOut;
315	    this.dotChunkOut = 0;
316	    return c;
317	  }
318	  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */
319	  tick(dt: number): number {
320	    let heal = 0;
321	    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {
322	      const left = time - dt;
323	      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);
324	      else this.active.set(t, left);
325	    }
326	    // ---- UpdateBuffs 正项（Player.cs:9640+）：Regeneration lifeRegen+=4（2 HP/s）、
327	    //      Honey :18955 lifeRegen+=2（1 HP/s）、狼人 :11496 lifeRegen+=1（0.5 HP/s）。
328	    //      注意原版这些都在 NaturalLifeRegen 的 DoT 段（:18779+）**之前**求值——
329	    //      DoT 的 if(lifeRegen>0) lifeRegen=0 会把它们整池清零（见下）----
330	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
331	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
332	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) {
333	      // 狼人 lifeRegen++（:11496）= 0.5 HP/s → 每 2 秒恒 1 HP（独立累加器，勿与篝火
334	      // 共用 campfireAccum/campfireHeal——曾误挂致篝火不在时狼人回复随强度归零/错峰）
335	      this.werewolfAccum += dt;
336	      if (this.werewolfAccum >= 2) { this.werewolfAccum -= 2; heal += 1; }
337	    }
338	    // ---- DoT 段（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911）：每个 DoT 先 if(lifeRegen>0)
339	    //      lifeRegen=0 再累加负项——恢复类 buff **不能**抵消 OnFire/Burning 等
340	    //      （曾直接相加，恢复池被 DoT 按净额吃掉）。分块弹出（DOT_CHUNK），
341	    //      净伤害记入 dotChunkOut 供 HurtLifeRegen 飘字消费 ----
342	    let dots = 0;
343	    for (const [t, hpPerSec] of ENV_DOTS) {
344	      if (!this.active.has(t)) continue;
345	      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;                       // :18781 等 if (lifeRegen > 0) lifeRegen = 0
346	      const d = this.rateAccum(t, hpPerSec, dt);
347	      dots += d;
348	      heal += d;
349	    }
350	    if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;
351	    // 蜂蜜对冲（Player.cs:18915-18922：lifeRegen<0 时 honey 额外 lifeRegen+4 = +2 HP/s，
352	    // 且对冲后钳回 ≤0）——DoT 系统已实装,补上 buff-system-port 备忘里标"暂缺"的分支
353	    if (heal < 0 && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {
354	      heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);
355	      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;
356	    }
357	    // ---- 光环正项（NaturalLifeRegen :18988-18993，位于 DoT 段之后——篝火/心灯
358	    //      的 lifeRegen 按原版可对冲 DoT）----
359	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Campfire)) {
360	      // 原版 lifeRegen+1 = 0.5 HP/s(Player.cs:18990/12244-12245)→ 每 2 秒 1 HP
361	      this.campfireAccum += dt;
362	      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) {
363	        this.campfireAccum -= 2;
364	        heal += this.campfireHeal;
365	      }
366	    }
367	    if (this.lanternHeal > 0) {
368	      this.lanternAccum += dt;
369	      if (this.lanternAccum >= 1) {
370	        this.lanternAccum -= 1;
371	        heal += this.lanternHeal;
372	      }
373	    }
374	    return heal;
375	  }
376	  /** 定额回复/伤害 buff 的独立累加器（Map 存每 buff 的秒余数;负速率=DoT）。
377	   *  separate=true 用独立累加器键(蜂蜜对冲与常规蜂蜜回复分账,避免共用余数)。
378	   *  负速率按 DOT_CHUNK 整块弹出（≈原版 lifeRegenCount 阈值分块）。已知偏差:
379	   *  原版 lifeRegenCount 是全局共享计数器,buff 到期后负余量仍残留(由后续
380	   *  DoT/自然回复对冲);本实现 per-buff 累加器到期即弃余数(<1 块,≤4 HP,一次性) */
381	  private rateLeft = new Map<string, number>();
382	  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
383	    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
384	    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
385	    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
386	    let heal = 0;
387	    if (hpPerSec >= 0) {
388	      while (left >= 1) { left -= 1; heal += 1; }
389	    } else {
390	      const chunk = DOT_CHUNK[t] ?? 1;
391	      // 原版 lifeRegenCount 是整数累加（-600/-120 阈值精确）;此处 dt 浮点积分
392	      // 在块边界会差 ~1e-15（如 1/3×15=4.999…）,补 epsilon 保证阈值时点对齐
393	      while (left <= -chunk + 1e-9) { left += chunk; heal -= chunk; }
394	    }
395	    this.rateLeft.set(key, left);
396	    return heal;
397	  }
398	  // ---- 属性聚合（幂等读取，不做副作用写入）----
399	  get moveSpeedMult(): number {
400	    let m = this.active.has(BuffType.Agility) ? 1.25 : 1;   // Swiftness moveSpeed += 0.25
401	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Burning)) m *= 0.5;         // Burning moveSpeed *= 0.5（:18886）
402	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Chilled)) m *= 0.75;        // Chilled moveSpeed *= 0.75（:25659-25661）
403	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Sunflower)) m *= 1.21;      // 向日葵 +0.1 再 ×1.1 两步复合（:10598）
404	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Weak)) m *= 0.9;            // 虚弱 moveSpeed -= 0.1（:11510）
405	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) m *= 1.05;      // 狼人 moveSpeed += 0.05（:11498）
406	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.KiteBuff)) m += 0.25;       // 风筝 moveSpeed += 0.25（:9614）
407	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.SugarRush)) m *= 1.2;       // 糖分冲刺 moveSpeed += 0.2（:9634）
408	    m += [0, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4][this.wellFedTier];               // 食物 moveSpeed += 0.2/0.3/0.4（:11532+）
409	    return m;
410	  }
411	  get defenseBonus(): number {
412	    let d = this.active.has(BuffType.Ironskin) ? 8 : 0;     // Ironskin statDefense += 8（1456）
413	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.CatBast)) d += 5;          // 猫堡垒 statDefense += 5（:9778）
414	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Tipsy)) d -= 4;            // 醉酒 statDefense -= 4（:11514）
415	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Weak)) d -= 4;              // 虚弱 statDefense -= 4（:11509）
416	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) d += 3;         // 狼人 statDefense += 3（:11497）
417	    d += [0, 2, 3, 4][this.wellFedTier];                    // 食物 statDefense += 2/3/4（:11524+）
418	    return d;
419	  }
420	  /** 魔力上限加成：水晶球预见 statManaMax2 += 20（:11481） */
421	  get manaMaxBonus(): number {
422	    return this.active.has(BuffType.Clairvoyance) ? 20 : 0;
423	  }
424	  /** 弹药箱 20% 不耗弹（PickAmmo :52746 rand.Next(5)==0） */
425	  get hasAmmoBox(): boolean {

427	  }
428	  // ---- 食物三档属性（Player.cs:11523-11570；同时多档激活时取最高档，与原版逐条累加
429	  // 等价——原版吃高档后低档不可能并存低于高档单独存在的情况）----
430	  /** 当前生效的食物档位：0 无 / 1 WellFed(26) / 2 WellFed2(206) / 3 WellFed3(207) */
431	  get wellFedTier(): 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 {
432	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed3)) return 3;
433	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed2)) return 2;
434	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.WellFed)) return 1;
435	    return 0;
436	  }

438	  get wellFedDamage(): number {

440	  }
441	  /** 全系暴击 +2/3/4（melee/magic/rangedCrit 同值,:11525+） */
442	  get wellFedCrit(): number {
443	    return [0, 2, 3, 4][this.wellFedTier];
444	  }
445	  /** 近战速度 +5/7.5/10%（meleeSpeed += ,:11528+） */

448	  }
449	  /** 镐速 +5/10/15%（pickSpeed -= ,:11534+）→ 挖掘倍率 */
450	  get wellFedPickMult(): number {
451	    return [1, 0.95, 0.9, 0.85][this.wellFedTier];
452	  }
453	  // ---- 风筝 buff 383（Player.cs:9612-9623）----
454	  /** kiteLuckLevel（:9618-9623）：clamp(buffTime/10800, 0, 3)。active 存秒，
455	   *  10800 tick = 180s 一档；无 buff = 0（luck += 档×0.1/3，RecalculateLuck :28675） */
456	  get kiteLuckLevel(): number {
457	    const left = this.active.get(BuffType.KiteBuff);
458	    if (left === undefined) return 0;
459	    return Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Math.floor(left / 180)));
460	  }

463	  get pickSpeedMult(): number {

471	  }

9	interface StatusEntry { buff: number; chance: number; durMin: number; durMax: number }

14	 *  189 Daybreak/203 BetsysCurse/204 Oiled/323 OnFire3/324 Frostburn2/337 TentacleSpike/
15	 *  344 BloodButcherer/375 Hemorrhage——Enemy 无对应 DoT/减益字段,命中静默跳过
16	 *  (表数据完整,字段补齐后自动生效) */
17	const SUPPORTED: Record<number, string> = {
18	  24: 'onFireT',     // On Fire!(NPC.cs:92619 lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s)
19	  20: 'poisonT',     // Poisoned(-12 → 6 HP/s,:92590)
20	  70: 'venomT',      // Venom(-60 → 30 HP/s,:92706)
21	  39: 'cursedT',     // Cursed Inferno(onFire2,-48 → 24 HP/s,:92690)
22	  31: 'confusedT',   // Confused(TargetClosest direction 反转,:78569)
23	  69: 'ichorT',      // Ichor(防御 -15;repo 近似=armorPen 7,Enemy.ts:4132)
24	  72: 'midasT',      // Midas(掉钱 ×1.10-1.51,Enemy.ts:4286 rollCoins)
25	};
26	
27	type StatusEnemy = { [field: string]: number | undefined };
28	
29	/** 命中结算侧调用:按投射物型号掷骰并施加 debuff(AddBuff max 合并语义)。
30	 *  duration 掷骰 Next(durMin, durMax+1) tick → 秒(/60) */
31	export function applyProjStatus(projId: number | undefined, enemy: StatusEnemy): void {
32	  if (projId === undefined || projId < 0) return;
33	  const entries = TABLE[String(projId)];
34	  if (!entries) return;
35	  for (const en of entries) {
36	    if (Math.random() >= en.chance) continue;
37	    const field = SUPPORTED[en.buff];
38	    if (!field) continue; // 未实装 buff,登记见上
39	    const ticks = en.durMin + Math.floor(Math.random() * (en.durMax - en.durMin + 1));
40	    const sec = ticks / 60;
41	    const cur = enemy[field] ?? 0;
42	    if (sec > cur) enemy[field] = sec;
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	/** 武器浸剂对【投射物近战族】的命中施加（StatusNPC:10559-10583 开头段:
47	 *  (melee||whip) && meleeEnchant>0 → 按药剂施加。近战挥砍侧同表在
48	 *  Game.ts 挥击结算内,此函数供 WeaponProj 四族(链球/悠悠球/长矛/回旋镖)共用)。
49	 *  时长 = Player.cs:6141-6171 Next(a,b) 秒,max 合并 */
50	export function applyMeleeImbue(
51	  buffs: { has: (b: never) => boolean },
52	  enemy: StatusEnemy,
53	  partyFx?: () => void,
54	): void {
55	  const has = (bt: BuffType): boolean => buffs.has(bt as unknown as never);
56	  const max = (field: string, v: number): void => {
57	    if (v > (enemy[field] ?? 0)) enemy[field] = v;
58	  };
59	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueVenom)) max('venomT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);
60	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueCursed)) max('cursedT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);
61	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueFire)) max('onFireT', 3 + Math.random() * 4);
62	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueGold)) max('midasT', 2);
63	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueIchor)) max('ichorT', 10 + Math.random() * 10);
64	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueNano)) max('confusedT', 1 + Math.random() * 3);
65	  if (has(BuffType.ImbuePoison)) max('poisonT', 5 + Math.random() * 5);
66	  if (has(BuffType.ImbueParty)) partyFx?.();
67	}

57	 */
58	export function liquidCollision(st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number, liquidType: number): boolean {
59	  const TS = 16;
60	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS));
61	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((px + pw - 0.01) / TS));
62	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS));
63	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor((py + ph - 0.01) / TS));
64	  for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
65	    for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
66	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
67	      if (st.liquid[idx] > 0 && st.liquidType[idx] === liquidType) return true;
68	    }
69	  }
70	  return false;
71	}
72	
73	/**
74	 * Collision.DrownCollision（Collision.cs:1385-1444）1:1——头部浸液判定。
75	 * 头部采样盒:X=px+pw/2 居中宽 min(10,pw),Y=py-2(gravDir==-1 时 +ph/2-6),
76	 * 高 min(12,ph)。命中条件:液体>0 且非岩浆(2)非微光(4)——**蜂蜜(3)也可淹**
77	 * ——且该格不在豁免行(gravDir==1 取扫描首行,否则末行-1;豁免行若
78	 * active+solid+非 solidTop 则不计,即头顶贴实心块时该行液体不算)。
79	 * 液面高度:drop=(256-liquid)/32,表面 Y=j*16+drop*2,有效高 16-trunc(drop*2)。
80	 * 偏差:tileSolidTop≈isPlatform(平台近似);includeSlopes 玩家调用不传,省略。
81	 */
82	export function drownCollision(
83	  st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number, gravDir: 1 | -1,
84	): boolean {
85	  const TS = 16;
86	  const num = Math.min(10, pw), num2 = Math.min(12, ph);
87	  const vx = px + pw / 2 - num / 2;
88	  const vy = py - 2 + (gravDir === -1 ? ph / 2 - 6 : 0);
89	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
90	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
91	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
92	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
93	  const topRow = gravDir === 1 ? j0 : j1 - 1; // 豁免判定行(原版 num4)
94	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
95	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
96	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
97	      const liq = st.liquid[idx], lt = st.liquidType[idx];
98	      if (liq <= 0 || lt === 2 || lt === 4) continue; // !lava && !shimmer
99	      if (j === topRow && st.isActive(i, j) && st.isSolid(i, j) && !st.isPlatform(i, j)) continue;
100	      const drop = (256 - liq) / 32;
101	      const surfY = j * TS + drop * 2;
102	      const surfH = TS - Math.trunc(drop * 2);
103	      if (vx + num > i * TS && vx < i * TS + TS && vy + num2 > surfY && vy < surfY + surfH) {
104	        return true;
105	      }
106	    }
107	  }
108	  return false;
109	}

114	 */
115	export function stickyTiles(st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number): HurtTile | null {
116	  const TS = 16;
117	  const bySheet = BY_SHEET;
118	  const WEB = bySheet.get(51) ?? -1;
119	  const LIVING = bySheet.get(229) ?? -1;
120	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
121	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
122	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
123	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
124	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
125	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
126	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
127	      if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;
128	      const t = st.type[idx];
129	      if (t !== WEB && !(t === LIVING && st.slope[idx] === 0)) continue;
130	      if (px + pw > i * TS && px < i * TS + TS && py + ph > j * TS && py < j * TS + 16.01) {
131	        return { type: t, x: i, y: j };
132	      }
133	    }
134	  }
135	  return null;
136	}
137	
138	/**
139	 * HurtTiles（Collision.cs:3128）:实体包围盒外扩 1 tile 扫描,返回首个可伤块。
140	 * 半砖下移 8px;窒息族缩盒 2px(num6);坡面块走原版 num7 双对面命中判定
141	 * （仅坡上薄侧接触时跳过——本作坡面数据少,按原版比较式移植）。
142	 */
143	export function hurtTiles(
144	  st: TileStore, px: number, py: number, pw: number, ph: number, fireWalk: boolean,
145	): HurtTile | null {
146	  const TS = 16;
147	  const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(px / TS) - 1);
148	  const i1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.floor((px + pw) / TS) + 2);
149	  const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py / TS) - 1);
150	  const j1 = Math.min(st.h - 40, Math.floor((py + ph) / TS) + 2);
151	  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
152	    for (let j = j0; j < j1; j++) {
153	      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
154	      if (!st.flags[idx]) continue;
155	      const t = st.type[idx];
156	      if (!canTileHurt(t, fireWalk)) continue;
157	      let ty = j * TS;
158	      let th = TS;
159	      if (st.half[idx]) { ty += 8; th -= 8; }
160	      const num6 = SUFFOCATE.has(t) ? 2 : 0;
161	      // 盒重叠（原版四向比较）
162	      if (px + pw - num6 < i * TS || px + num6 > i * TS + TS
163	        || py + ph - num6 < ty - 0.5 || py + num6 > ty + th + 0.5) continue;
164	      // 坡面:实体须与坡的"厚"侧重叠（num7==2 = 全在薄侧 → 跳过）
165	      const slope = st.slope[idx];
166	      if (slope > 0) {
167	        if (num6 > 0) continue; // 窒息族不与坡面交互（原版同款）
168	        let num7 = 0;
169	        if ((slope === 1 || slope === 4) && px > i * TS) num7++;          // 右高
170	        if ((slope === 2 || slope === 3) && px + pw < i * TS + TS) num7++; // 左高
171	        if ((slope === 3 || slope === 4) && py > ty) num7++;               // 下高(倒坡)
172	        if ((slope === 1 || slope === 2) && py + ph < ty + th) num7++;     // 上高
173	        if (num7 === 2) continue;
174	      }
175	      return { type: t, x: i, y: j };
176	    }
177	  }
178	  return null;
179	}

383	  seekDirX(player: Player | null): number {

469	    this.prevX = this.x; this.prevY = this.y;
470	    // 联机傀儡（房主权威快照驱动）：无本地 AI——P3.2 插值/外推推进 + 动画计时，
471	    // 打击/拾取等交互经 entityNet 上报房主结算
472	    if (this.netPuppet) {
473	      this.aiT++;
474	      this.animT++;
475	      this.netPuppetStep();
476	      this.onGround = Math.abs(this.vy) < 0.6;
477	      if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.05) this.walkCycleT += Math.abs(this.vx);
478	      if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
479	      this.pushHistory();   // 傀儡无本地 AI 但拖尾渲染同样需要历史位置
480	      return;
481	    }
482	    this.aiT++;
483	    if (this.iframes > 0) this.iframes--;
484	    if (this.justHitT > 0) this.justHitT--;
485	    this.lightRGB = null; // 点光由 AI 每帧重写（不残留上一帧状态）
486	    this.lightRGBAt = null;

489	    if (this.whipProcT > 0) this.whipProcT--;
490	    if (this.squash !== 0) this.squash *= 0.85;

668	    // 入水溅落声（与主角一致）：音量随与主角距离衰减，Boss 更响
669	    const eli = game.world.store.liquid[game.world.store.idx(
670	      Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE),
671	    )];
672	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
673	    this.inWater = eli > 80;
674	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && hasPlayer) {
675	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
676	      const vol = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, 1 - d / (TILE * 45))) * (this.def.boss ? 1.2 : 0.9);
677	      game.playSfx('splash', vol);
678	    }

680	    // 岩浆接触（NPC.cs:94520-94545）：30t 独立冷却（immune[255],不受普通受击 iframes 影响）,
681	    // onFire 7s + 50 直伤;lavaImmune 族（NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE,SetDefaults 提取）完全豁免
682	    if (this.vanillaId === null || !NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE.has(this.vanillaId)) {
683	      if (this.lavaCd > 0) this.lavaCd--;
684	      const inLava = liquidCollision(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 2);
685	      if (inLava) {
686	        this.lavaWet = true;
687	        if (this.lavaCd <= 0) {
688	          this.lavaCd = 30;
689	          this.onFireT = Math.max(this.onFireT, 7); // AddBuff(24,420)
690	          this.hurt(50, 0, 0, game);                // StrikeNPCNoInteraction(50)
691	          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF9A3C', 10, 1.6, { life: 30, grav: -0.05 });
692	        }
693	      } else {
694	        this.lavaWet = false;
695	      }
696	    } else {
697	      this.lavaWet = false;
698	    }
699	    // 入水熄灭 onFire（NPC.cs:94284 TryRemovingWaterPerishableEffects:水湿(非岩浆)→DelBuff 24）
700	    if (this.inWater && !this.lavaWet) this.onFireT = 0;
701	    // 微光接触（NPC.cs:94268-94274）：WetCollision 盒命中微光 → shimmerWet +
702	    // TryAddingRepeatedBuff(353,100)；buff 353 → shimmering（:93313-93321）→
703	    // shimmerTransparency +0.01/t（:92468-92483）>0.9 → GetShimmered。离池衰减
704	    // （:92484-92500）视觉侧未接（GAP），此处只保留转化触发计时。
705	    // 入口门（NPC.cs:17773）：buffImmune[353] = ShimmerImmunity[type]——免表 NPC
706	    // （史莱姆王/石巨人/史莱姆皇后/城镇宠物/事件军…，isNpcShimmerImmune）不进
707	    // shimmering；非免表 Boss（克苏鲁之眼 4 等）原版照进，GetShimmered 无表项
708	    // 时为空操作（npcGetShimmered 同语义提前返回）
709	    if (this.vanillaId !== null && !isNpcShimmerImmune(this.vanillaId)) {
710	      if (liquidCollision(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 4)) {
711	        this.shimmerT += 1;
712	        if (this.shimmerT > 90) this.npcGetShimmered(game);
713	      } else if (this.shimmerT > 0) {
714	        this.shimmerT -= 1;
715	      }
716	    }
717	    // onFire DoT（NPC.cs:92619:lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s,无防御结算）+ 火尘粒子（:92187）
718	    if (this.onFireT > 0) {
719	      this.onFireT -= dt;
720	      this.onFireDoT += 4 * dt;
721	      if (this.onFireDoT >= 1) {
722	        const n = Math.floor(this.onFireDoT);
723	        this.onFireDoT -= n;
724	        this.hp -= n;
725	        this.hpBarT = 240;
726	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game); // 走标准死亡管线(掉落/分裂/联机)
727	      }
728	      if (Math.random() < 0.75) {
729	        game.spawnParticles(
730	          this.x + Math.random() * this.w, this.y + Math.random() * this.h,
731	          Math.random() < 0.7 ? '#FF9A3C' : '#FFD24A', 1, 0.8,
732	          { life: 26 + Math.random() * 14, grav: -0.06, damp: 0.96 });
733	      }
734	    }

736	    // ---- 武器浸剂 debuff 滴血（R2，NPC.cs:92585/92697/92708 DoT 同 onFire 模式） ----
737	    if (this.poisonT > 0) { this.poisonT -= dt; this.poisonDoT += 6 * dt; }
738	    if (this.venomT > 0) { this.venomT -= dt; this.venomDoT += 30 * dt; }
739	    if (this.cursedT > 0) { this.cursedT -= dt; this.cursedDoT += 24 * dt; }
740	    for (const [tKey, dKey] of [['poisonT', 'poisonDoT'], ['venomT', 'venomDoT'], ['cursedT', 'cursedDoT']] as const) {
741	      const acc = this[dKey];
742	      if (acc >= 1) {
743	        const n = Math.floor(acc);
744	        this[dKey] -= n;
745	        this.hp -= n;
746	        this.hpBarT = 240;
747	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game);
748	      }
749	    }
750	    if (this.ichorT > 0) this.ichorT -= dt;
751	    if (this.midasT > 0) this.midasT -= dt;
752	    // confused（buff 31）存量倒数；反向语义在各 AI 的索敌点（seekDirX）按

754	    if (this.confusedT > 0) this.confusedT -= dt;

756	    // 接触伤害（带上攻击者引用，供荆棘反伤）。damage=0（小动物等友好生物）不触发——
757	    // 否则 damagePlayer(0) 仍会击退/闪红，表现为"兔子撞我有攻击性"
758	    if (hasPlayer && this.aabbOverlaps(player) && this.def.damage > 0) {
759	      // 蜂蜜史莱姆（NPC 1 且 ai[1]=1125，Player.cs:30902-30908 接触分支最前）：
760	      // AddBuff(48 Honey, 1800t=30s) 后 continue——**免接触伤害/击退/无敌帧**，
761	      // 语义是"蹭一身蜜"而非攻击（此前仅 SLIME_ITEM_COLORS 染色，接触照常掉血）
762	      if (this.vanillaId === 1 && this.ai1 === 1125) {
763	        player.buffs?.apply(BuffType.Honey, 30);
764	      } else {

767	    }

769	    // Despawn 系统（原版 CheckActive, NPC.cs:78669-78798 + EncourageDespawn :7228-7235）：

210	        //   ModifyTaggedHit tag 加伤+TagCrit 暴击（WhipTagEffect.cs:58-70）、OnTaggedHit
211	        //   （黑收成 916）、proc 窗口首跳（TagEffectState.cs:216-243）。随从弹无基础
212	        //   暴击（原版 summon 系 crit 仅来自 TagCrit）——4% 基础暴击只留给玩家远程弹）
213	        let dmg = this.damage;
214	        let crit: boolean;
215	        if (this.whipTagShot) {
216	          const en2 = e as unknown as import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged;
217	          // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价：额外一跳落在被命中敌上）
218	          en2.hurtFx = (d: number) => { e.hurt(d, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, -2, game); };
219	          const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en2, dmg, game,
220	            (el) => (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, 'projectiles'),
221	            SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.h / 2);
222	          dmg = res.dmg;
223	          crit = res.crit;
224	        } else {
225	          // 暴击：基 4% + spawn 注入的系暴击/item.crit（P:2300-2304/P:25230-25232）
226	          crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);
227	          if (crit) dmg *= 2;
228	        }
229	        if (this.ignite) {
230	          const en3 = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number };
231	          en3.onFireT = Math.max(en3.onFireT ?? 0, 5); // AddBuff(24,300)=5s
232	        }
233	        // 命中 debuff（StatusNPC:10555 表驱动——火枪弹 2 33% OnFire 180t 等,按型号掷骰）
234	        applyProjStatus(this.projId, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
235	        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
236	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
237	        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game);
238	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
239	        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
240	        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----
241	        // 吸血鬼飞刀 304 → vampireHeal（:12879-12882，!moonLeech 门）；
242	        // 魔法弹 + 幽灵套 ghostHeal → ghostHeal（:12883-12888，canGhostHeal 全 true
243	        // 近似——雕像产怪 flag 未实装）。ghostHurt（幽灵伤害头 156）未接，登记。
244	        // numHits = 含本次的命中数（原版 StrikeNPC 先自增，hitSet.size 此时已含本敌）
245	        if (this.projId === 304) {
246	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('vampire', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y);
247	        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
248	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('ghost', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y, true);
249	        }
250	        if (--this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
251	      }
252	    }
253	    // 城镇 NPC 受击(原版 Projectile.Damage 对 friendly NPC 同样生效——杀裁缝师
254	    // 召骷髅王即此链);穿透/销毁语义与敌怪一致
255	    if (hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2)
256	      && --this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
257	  }
258	
259	  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。

```

16	/** 弹幕与实体 AABB 重叠 */
17	export function projOverlap(ax: number, ay: number, aw: number, ah: number, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
18	  return ax < e.x + e.w && ax + aw > e.x && ay < e.y + e.h && ay + ah > e.y;

22	/** 城镇 NPC 受击判定(玩家弹幕/敌怪弹幕共用)。门禁照原版两分支:

24	 *    仅向导 22(装备向导巫毒娃娃 267→player.killGuide)/裁缝师 54(裁缝娃 1307→
25	 *    killClothier)可被玩家弹幕/近战伤害;其余城镇 NPC 玩家武器一律无效
26	 *  - 'hostile'(敌怪弹/陷阱弹):原版 flag2 分支(hostile && victim.friendly)恒命中
27	 *  TownNPC.hurt 自带 8t 无敌帧去重;kbx=0 时按相对方位自动击退方向。 */
28	export function hitTownNpcs(
29	  self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },
30	  game: GameHooks,
31	  dmg: number,
32	  kbx = 0,
33	  source: 'playerProj' | 'hostile' = 'playerProj',
34	): boolean {
35	  const list = (game as unknown as { npcs?: () => unknown[] }).npcs?.() ?? [];
36	  // player 可为 null(测试 fakeHooks/导入早期帧)——加空守卫
37	  const eq = (game.player as unknown as { equipStats?: { killGuide?: boolean; killClothier?: boolean } } | null)
38	    ?.equipStats;
39	  let any = false;
40	  for (const ent of list) {
41	    const n = ent as {
42	      x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
43	      hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean;
44	      bound?: boolean; vanillaId?: number;
45	    };
46	    if (n.dead || typeof n.hurt !== 'function') continue;
47	    if (n.bound) continue; // 被缚待救不可打(近似;原版 bound 型有独立受击语义)
48	    if (!projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, n)) continue;
49	    if (source === 'playerProj') {
50	      // 巫毒窄门(:11970-11972):22→killGuide / 54→killClothier,其余 false
51	      const vid = n.vanillaId ?? -1;
52	      const doll = vid === 22 ? eq?.killGuide : vid === 54 ? eq?.killClothier : false;
53	      if (!doll) continue;
54	    }
55	    const kb = kbx !== 0 ? kbx : Math.sign(n.x + n.w / 2 - (self.x + self.w / 2)) * 2 || 1;
56	    if (n.hurt(dmg, kb, 0, game)) any = true;
57	  }
58	  return any;
59	}

87	 *    受击音/伤害数字/饰品触发)。
88	 *  原版敌弹命中【不消耗穿透】(:13811-13829 仅 435/436/437/681/682 特判递减),
89	 *  弹体照常飞行——调用方不得据此销毁弹体。
90	 *  返回是否【实际造成伤害】(iframes/闪避吞掉时 false,对应原版 Hurt 返回 0;
91	 *  StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予以此为准,:13798-13800)。 */
92	export function hitPlayer(
93	  self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number },
94	  game: GameHooks,
95	  dmg: number,
96	): boolean {
97	  if (dmg <= 0) return false;
98	  const p = game.player;
99	  if (p.dead) return false;
100	  if (p.iframes > 0) return false; // 原版 :13732 player.immune 门禁（General 槽）
101	  if (!projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, p)) return false;
102	  const ifr = p.iframes;
103	  // ★ 玩家受伤难度乘区（Projectile.cs:13770，Damage_EVP 段）：
104	  //   `if (!reflected) num3 = (int)(num3 * hostileDamageScaling.Sample(Main.Difficulty));`
105	  //   hostileDamageScaling = GameDifficultyData.HostileProjectileDamageMultiplier
106	  //   （Projectile.cs:264/544：旅程 0.5 / 经典 1 / 专家 2 / 大师 3）。
107	  //   NPC 接触伤害不走这里——其难度缩放在生成时由 NPC.ScaleStats 完成
108	  //   （Enemy.def.damage 已是缩放值），在此再乘会双重缩放。
109	  //   （本函数调用方均为 hostile 弹幕，无 reflected 路径——反射弹转 friendly 打怪）
110	  game.damagePlayer(hostileProjectileDamage(dmg, mainDifficulty(game.world)), self.x + self.w / 2, self.y + self.h / 2);
111	  return p.iframes > ifr; // damage 落地才置无敌帧（闪避不置，对应 Hurt 返回 0）
112	}

114	// ================= 弹幕反射管线（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060 + NPC.cs:67003-67059） =================
115	
116	/** 可反射 aiStyle 白名单（Projectile.CanBeReflected，Projectile.cs:20224）：
117	 *  1 箭/子弹直飞弹、2 抛物线投掷物、8/21/24/28/29/131 各特化直线弹。
118	 *  另有 type 728（超级星星）/955（坠落星）无条件可反射（:20220）。 */
119	const REFLECTABLE_AI_STYLES = new Set([1, 2, 8, 21, 24, 28, 29, 131]);
120	
121	/** 可反射弹体（调用方自报运动学字段；hostile/reflected/damage 参与门禁） */
122	export interface ReflectableProj {
123	  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
124	  vx: number; vy: number;
125	  damage: number;
126	  hostile?: boolean;
127	  /** 已反射标记（本仓显式防重复；原版靠 friendly→hostile 翻转天然拦截二次反射） */
128	  reflected?: boolean;
129	}
130	
131	/** 原版 Projectile.CanBeReflected（Projectile.cs:20216-20230）：
132	 *  active && friendly && !hostile && damage>0，且 type∈{728,955} 或
133	 *  aiStyle∈{1,2,8,21,24,28,29,131}。本仓 friendly 无字段——非 hostile 即友方。 */
134	export function projCanBeReflected(
135	  projId: number,
136	  proj: Pick<ReflectableProj, 'hostile' | 'damage'>,
137	): boolean {
138	  if (proj.hostile || !(proj.damage > 0)) return false;
139	  if (projId === 728 || projId === 955) return true;
140	  return REFLECTABLE_AI_STYLES.has(projectileData(projId)?.aiStyle ?? -1);
141	}
142	
143	/** 原版 NPC.ReflectProjectile（NPC.cs:67036-67059）：
144	 *  - 音效 Item150 + 尘 31×3（velocity×0.3）；
145	 *  - reflected=true、friendly=false、hostile=true（owner 不变——"朝发射者"
146	 *    方向读 Main.player[owner]，本仓单主角恒为 game.player）；
147	 *  - 速度 = normalize(随机方向×原速 + 朝发射者×20) × 原速（:67047-67055，
148	 *    即随机散射偏向回打玩家；原速取 oldVelocity.Length，此处用当前速度近似）；
149	 *  - damage ÷2 两次（int 语义 ≈ ÷4）+ penetrate=1（穿透归一，穿透字段的
150	 *    清理由调用方按各自存储执行）。 */
151	export function reflectProjectile(proj: ReflectableProj, game: GameHooks): void {
152	  const cx = proj.x + proj.w / 2, cy = proj.y + proj.h / 2;
153	  // Item_150 未入音效提取库（public/sounds 无此 wav）：playSfxFile 未命中静默，
154	  // 回退 tink（Tink_0/1 金属弹开近似）——素材补齐后自动切真声
155	  if (!game.playSfxFile?.('Item_150', 1, cx, cy)) game.playSfx('tink', 0.9, cx, cy);
156	  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#D8D8E0', 3, 0.6, { life: 24, damp: 0.9 });
157	  proj.reflected = true;
158	  proj.hostile = true;
159	  // 反射向量（NPC.cs:67047-67055）
160	  const p = game.player;
161	  const speed = Math.hypot(proj.vx, proj.vy);
162	  let ox = p.cx - cx, oy = p.cy - cy;
163	  const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);
164	  if (od > 0.001) { ox /= od; oy /= od; }
165	  else if (speed > 0.001) { ox = -proj.vx / speed; oy = -proj.vy / speed; } // 重合退化:原路弹回
166	  else { ox = 0; oy = -1; }
167	  // 随机方向 = Main.rand.Next(-100,101) 双轴归一（此处 [-1,1) 均匀近似）
168	  const rx = Math.random() * 2 - 1, ry = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
169	  let dx = rx * speed + ox * 20, dy = ry * speed + oy * 20;
170	  const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
171	  if (dl > 0.001) { proj.vx = (dx / dl) * speed; proj.vy = (dy / dl) * speed; }
172	  else { proj.vx = ox * 6; proj.vy = oy * 6; } // 零速退化:低速直飞回发射者
173	  // 伤害衰减（int 连除两次）+ penetrate=1（:67056-67058）
174	  proj.damage = Math.floor(Math.floor(proj.damage / 2) / 2);
175	}
176	
177	/** 弹体命中敌怪分支的反射门禁 + 执行（原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060：
178	 *  flag6 = targetNPC.reflectsProjectiles（getGoodWorld 星弹特例未接入——运行时
179	 *  无 FTW 标志），命中重叠本身即 NPC.CanReflectProjectile 的 Hitbox 相交）。
180	 *  返回 true = 已反射，调用方跳过本次伤害结算并结束本帧命中迭代
181	 *  （对应原版 keepIterating=false; return）。 */
182	export function tryReflectProjectile(
183	  proj: ReflectableProj,
184	  projId: number,
185	  e: { reflectsProjectiles?: boolean },
186	  game: GameHooks,
187	): boolean {
188	  if (!e.reflectsProjectiles) return false;
189	  if (proj.reflected) return false; // 显式防重复（原版由 hostile 翻转天然拦截）
190	  if (!projCanBeReflected(projId, proj)) return false;
191	  reflectProjectile(proj, game);
192	  return true;
193	}
194	
195	/** 敌弹命中玩家的状态授予(原版 Projectile.StatusPlayer :11450+,仅在
196	 *  hitPlayer 返回 true 即 Hurt>0 后调用)。本仓库已实装弹型登记:
197	 *   348 FrostWave —— Chilled(46) 300/600t(:11554-11557) + Frozen(47)
198	 *                    概率 20/40/60t 三级(:11558-11567)
199	 *   349 FrostShard —— Chilled(46) 1/3→600t、否则 1/2→300t(:11580-11588)
200	 *  其余弹型的 debuff 链(毒/诅咒/灵液/暗影焰等)随对应批次逐条接入。 */
201	export function statusPlayer(game: GameHooks, projId: number): void {
202	  const p = game.player;
203	  switch (projId) {
204	    case 348:
205	      p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Chilled, (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 300 : 600) / 60);
206	      if (Math.random() < 2 / 3) {
207	        const r = Math.random();
208	        const t = r < 1 / 16 ? 60 : r < 1 / 12 ? 40 : r < 1 / 8 ? 20 : 0;
209	        if (t > 0) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Frozen, t / 60);
210	      }
211	      break;
212	    case 349: {
213	      const r = Math.random();
214	      if (r < 1 / 3) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Chilled, 600 / 60);
215	      else if (r < 0.5) p.buffs.apply(BuffType.Chilled, 300 / 60);
216	      break;
217	    }
218	    default:
219	      break;
220	  }
221	}
222

545	  get attackSpeedMult(): number {

550	      * (1 + 0.1 * this.beetleOrbs)                          // 甲虫攻击球攻速（:10071+）

556	  damageMult(kind: 'melee' | 'ranged' | 'magic' | 'summon'): number {
557	    const st = this.equipStats;
558	    if (kind === 'summon') {
559	      // minionDamage 链（Player.cs GetWeaponDamageMultiplier summon 分支）：仅四系通用
560	      // 来源（怒气/食物）+ 召唤装备链；MagicPower/Archery 等 class 来源不吃
561	      let m = 1 + st.minionDmg;
562	      if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Rage)) m += 0.1;
563	      if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.BrainOfConfusionBuff)) m += 0.1; // BOC 321（:9639-9646）
564	      m += this.buffs.wellFedDamage;
565	      m += 0.15 * this.nebula[0];        // 星云伤害 179-181 每级四系 +15%（:10118-10124）
566	      return m;
567	    }
568	    let m = 1 + (kind === 'melee' ? st.dmgMelee : kind === 'ranged' ? st.dmgRanged : st.dmgMagic);
569	    if (kind === 'magic') {
570	      m += this.buffs.magicDmgBonus;            // MagicPower +20%（:9669）
571	      m *= this.buffs.manaSickMagicMult;        // 魔力眩晕最高 -25% 线性恢复（:10003/:25616）
572	    }
573	    if (kind === 'ranged') m *= this.buffs.arrowDmgMult;   // Archery ×1.1（:9712,ranged 近义）
574	    // R1 药水：怒气(117)全系 +10%（:9947）；醉酒(25)近战 +10%（:11513）
575	    if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Rage)) m += 0.1;
576	    if (kind === 'melee') {
577	      if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Tipsy)) m += 0.1;
578	      if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) m += 0.051;      // 狼人（:11488）
579	      if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Weak)) m -= 0.051;         // 虚弱（:11506）
580	      m += 0.1 * this.beetleOrbs;                              // 甲虫攻击球每颗 +10%（:10071+）
581	      if (this.heldPsychoKnife) m += (1 - this.stealth) * 3;   // 变态刀潜行（:25487）
582	    }
583	    m += this.buffs.wellFedDamage;   // 食物三档全系 +5/7.5/10%（:11527+）
584	    m += 0.15 * this.nebula[0];      // 星云四系 +15%/层
585	    return m;
586	  }
587	  /** 远程分道乘区（Player.cs:3820-3824 bowEffectiveDamage 拆分）：
588	   *  箭 = (rangedDamage + 箭袋stack) × Archery × 蘑菇矿箭头 1.12 × 潜行增伤；
589	   *  弹/火箭 = rangedDamage × 对应蘑菇矿头 1.12 × 潜行；其他（飞镖等）= rangedDamage */
590	  rangedDamageMult(kind: 'arrow' | 'bullet' | 'rocket' | 'other'): number {
591	    const st = this.equipStats;
592	    const base = this.damageMult('ranged'); // rangedDamage 全链（装备+四系 buff）
593	    const stealthBonus = 1 + (1 - this.stealth) * (st.vortexSet ? 0.8 : st.shroomiteSet ? 0.6 : 0);
594	    if (kind === 'arrow') {
595	      // bowEffectiveDamage :3820 = (rangedDamage/arrowMult + stack) × arrowMult²
596	      //   = (rangedDamage + stack×arrowMult) × arrowMult——stack 段平方差异在此
597	      const arrowMult = this.buffs.arrowDmgMult * (st.shroomiteType === 'arrow' ? 1.12 : 1);
598	      return (base + st.arrowStack * arrowMult) * arrowMult * stealthBonus;
599	    }
600	    if (kind === 'bullet') return base * (st.shroomiteType === 'bullet' ? 1.12 : 1) * stealthBonus;
601	    if (kind === 'rocket') return base * (st.shroomiteType === 'rocket' ? 1.12 : 1) * stealthBonus;
602	    return base;
603	  }
604	  /** 手持变态刀 3106（潜行判定用） */
605	  get heldPsychoKnife(): boolean {
606	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[this.inv.heldItem()?.id ?? -1];
607	    const vid = d?.vid ?? (d?.key?.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
608	    return vid === 3106;
609	  }
610	  /** 潜行 aggro（:25518/:25557/:25491：蘑菇矿 -750×(1-s) / 星璇 -1200 / 变态刀 -750）
611	   *  ——敌人索敌距离缩短（NPC.cs:78375 dist -= aggro）；各 AI 家族索敌门消费见后续批 */
612	  get aggro(): number {
613	    const s1 = 1 - this.stealth;
614	    if (this.equipStats.vortexSet) return -Math.round(1200 * s1);
615	    if (this.equipStats.shroomiteSet) return -Math.round(750 * s1);
616	    if (this.heldPsychoKnife) return -Math.round(750 * s1);

1	// 配饰/盔甲数值效果运行时（tools/extract-equip-prefix.mjs → vanilla-accfx.json）。

4	import fxJson from './vanilla-accfx.json';
5	import { ITEM_DEFS } from './items';
6	
7	export interface AccFx {
8	  life?: number;      // lifeRegen += N（每半秒 N/2 HP）
9	  mana?: number;      // statManaMax2 += N
10	  def?: number;       // statDefense += N
11	  move?: number;      // moveSpeed += X
12	  meleeSpd?: number;  // meleeSpeed += X
13	  runSpeed?: number;  // accRunSpeed = X（基础 maxRunSpeed=3）
14	  dmgMelee?: number;
15	  dmgRanged?: number;
16	  dmgMagic?: number;
17	  dmgAll?: number;
18	  critMelee?: number;
19	  critRanged?: number;
20	  critMagic?: number;
21	  // ---- 行为型（二轮）----
22	  extraFall?: number;    // 摔伤豁免高度
23	  lavaMax?: number;      // 岩浆免疫 tick（420=7s）
24	  armorPen?: number;     // 护甲穿透
25	  rocketBoots?: number;  // 飞行档 1-5（1=基础火箭靴 30t/格，2+=15t/格）
26	  autoJump?: number;     // bool（值=1/undefined，JSON 序列化 null→parseFloat→NaN? 见提取器）
27	  fireWalk?: number;     // 陨石/狱岩灼伤免疫
28	  noKnockback?: number;  // 击退免疫（钴护盾族）
29	  noFallDmg?: number;    // 摔伤免疫（幸运马掌族）
30	  desertBoots?: number;
31	  waterWalk?: number;    // 水面行走
32	  waterWalk2?: number;
33	  jumpBoost?: number;    // 气球跳跃增强
34	  panic?: number;        // 受击移速爆发（恐慌项链）
35	  starCloak?: number;    // 受击落星（星星斗篷）
36	  carpet?: number;       // 飞毯（水平滑翔，待滑翔系统）
37	  jumpOpts?: string[];   // 多段跳选项（Cloud/Blizzard/Sandstorm/Fart/Tsunami/…）
38	  // ---- 三轮（冲刺/闪避/蜜蜂）----
39	  dashType?: number;     // 1=忍者足具/水晶刺客 2=克苏鲁护盾（冲撞带伤害）
40	  blackBelt?: number;    // 10% 闪避（Player.Hurt :9772）
41	  bees?: number;         // 蜂窝/蜂巢背带：受击放蜂
42	  spikedBoots?: number;  // 攀壁钉鞋（贴墙滑降）
43	  // ---- 五轮（残余全量）----
44	  flipper?: number;      // 脚蹼：游泳提速
45	  jumpSpd?: number;      // jumpSpeedBoost（蛙腿族 +1.6）
46	  longInvince?: number;  // 无敌帧翻倍（十字项链/星星面纱）
47	  treasureMagnet?: number; // 宝藏磁石：拾取范围 +
48	  goldRing?: number;     // 金戒指族：钱币拾取范围
49	  luckyCoin?: number;    // 幸运币：近战命中掉钱
50	  discount?: number;     // 折扣卡：商店/重铸 8 折
51	  quiver?: number;       // 魔法箭袋族
52	  moltenQuiver?: number; // 熔箭袋 4002：木箭→火矢 +2 伤（:52700）
53	  arrowDmg?: number;     // 箭伤加成（+0.1/件）
54	  ammoSave?: number;     // 20% 概率不耗弹
55	  huntressSave?: number; // 10% 概率不耗弹
56	  kbGlove?: number;      // 力量手套族：近战击退翻倍
57	  autoReuseGlove?: number; // 力量手套族：近战武器自动连挥（Player.TryAllowingItemReuse :52040-52042）
58	  yoyoString?: number;   // 悠悠球线：射程 +
59	  tileSpeed?: number;    // 采矿/放置加速
60	  wallSpeed?: number;    // 铺墙加速
61	  tileRange?: number;    // 工具/放置射程 +
62	  skyStone?: number;     // 天界石族：全属性复合（:12558）
63	  counterWeight?: number; // 配重球（悠悠球命中触发）
64	  manaMagnet?: number;   // 魔力磁石：魔力星吸附范围 +
65	  pStone?: number;       // 贤者之石（药水 CD -25%）
66	  magicCuffs?: number;   // 魔力手铐：受伤回蓝（raw damage 1:1）
67	  nightWolf?: number;    // 月亮符：夜晚 Werewolf buff(28)（melee+5.1%伤/+2暴/+5.1%速）
68	  brainOfConfusion?: number; // 混乱之脑：受击 1/6 → buff321（三系+10暴+召唤+10%伤）
69	  manaFlower?: number;   // 魔力花族：耗魔 -8% + 魔力不足自动喝蓝
70	  // ---- 魔力回复直值（Player.ApplyEquipFunctional :14379-14383，仅魔力斗篷 982）----
71	  manaRegenBonus?: number;       // manaRegenBonus += N（每帧产额加成）
72	  manaRegenDelayBonus?: number;  // manaRegenDelayBonus += X（惩罚期额外衰减/tick）
73	  // ---- 呼吸族（行为型，见 BEHAVIOR_FX）----
74	  divingHelm?: number;  // accDivingHelm：breathCDMax ×6（潜水头盔/三件潜水装备）
75	  merman?: number;      // accMerman：水中不耗气并人鱼化（月亮/天界/海神贝壳）
76	  arcticDivingGear?: number; // 1861 极地潜水装备：专家雪原冷水 Chilled 免疫（:27692）
77	  jellyfishGlow?: number;    // 1860 水母潜水装备：浸水发光 1.8/0.4/1.2（:14682-14688）
78	}
79	
80	const FX = fxJson as Record<string, AccFx>;
81	
82	// 呼吸族行为表：原版 if-chain 行为型效果，提取器不进 json，代码层补齐（生成数据勿手改）。
83	// 268 走 GrantArmorBenefits（Player.cs:12829-12831）；394/1860/1861 走
84	// ApplyEquipFunctional（:14652/:14689/:14701，divingHelm 与 flipper 并存）；
85	// 497/861/3110 → accMerman（:15019/:14587/:14855）。
86	const BEHAVIOR_FX: Record<string, AccFx> = {
87	  // 力量手套族 autoReuseGlove（Player.ApplyEquipFunctional if-chain：
88	  // 211 猛爪手套 :14559-14562 / 897 强力手套 :14753-14756 / 1343 机械手套 :14761-14764 /
89	  // 936 烈火手套 :14823-14826 / 3992 泰坦手套 :13995-13999——各只置旗 + meleeSpeed += 0.12，
90	  // 数值段在 vanilla-accfx.json 已有，此处补布尔行为）
91	  '211': { autoReuseGlove: 1 },
92	  '897': { autoReuseGlove: 1 },
93	  '1343': { autoReuseGlove: 1 },
94	  '936': { autoReuseGlove: 1 },
95	  '3992': { autoReuseGlove: 1 },
96	  '268': { divingHelm: 1 },
97	  '394': { divingHelm: 1 },
98	  '1860': { divingHelm: 1, jellyfishGlow: 1 },   // 浸水 AddLight(1.8,0.4,1.2)（:14682-14688）
99	  '1861': { divingHelm: 1, arcticDivingGear: 1 }, // +arcticDivingGear（:14701；iceSkate 移动系未实装）
100	  '497': { merman: 1 },
101	  '861': { merman: 1, nightWolf: 1 },   // Moon Shell：accMerman + wolfAcc（:14585-14591）
102	  '3110': { merman: 1, nightWolf: 1 },  // Celestial Shell：同（:14853-14859；json 的 skyStone 保留）
103	};
104	
105	const FX_BY_IID = (() => {
106	  const m = new Map<number, AccFx>();
107	  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
108	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
109	    if (!d) continue;
110	    const vid = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
111	    if (vid < 0) continue;
112	    const json = FX[String(vid)];
113	    const extra = BEHAVIOR_FX[String(vid)];
114	    const fx = json || extra ? { ...json, ...extra } : null;
115	    if (fx) m.set(i, fx);
116	  }
117	  return m;
118	})();
119	
120	export function accFxOfInternal(id: number): AccFx | null {
121	  return FX_BY_IID.get(id) ?? null;
122	}

export function findShimmerFreeSpot(
  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,
  fallbackTx?: number, fallbackTy?: number,
): { x: number; y: number } | null {
  const clear = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
    for (let j = y - 2; j <= y; j++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
      const i = st.idx(x, j);

242	        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
243	        const dmg = this.dmgNow; // 当前鞭伤（已含召唤乘区+护穿；衰减链前）
244	        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(p.x - this.x) * this.knockback, -1, game);

249	          e as unknown as Record<string, number>,

252	        // 衰减链（:12604-12678）：每次命中后鞭 damage 字段 ×falloff
253	        this.dmgNow = Math.max(1, Math.round(this.dmgNow * (WHIP_FALLOFF[this.projId] ?? 1)));

261	        //  与期望一致（TagEffectState.cs:170-176），本次命中刚写入 → 恒真
262	        if (WHIP_PROC_ITEMS.has(this.itemId) && (this.itemId === 5480 || !this.procEnabled)) {
263	          this.procEnabled = true;
264	          e.whipProcT = 240;
265	          e.whipProcItem = this.itemId;
266	        }

268	        //  + SpawnConstellationStarsTowards :13869-13881）——伤 = 本次命中伤×0.33，
269	        //  沿鞭每 3 个控制点一颗、垂直曲线方向飞出
270	        if (this.projId === 1034 && this.localAI0 === 0) {
271	          this.localAI0 = 1;
272	          const starDmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 0.33));
273	          const add = (el: unknown, b: string) =>
274	            (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, y: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, b);
275	          for (let i = 2; i < pts.length - 1; i += 3) {

278	            const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
279	            // 垂直方向（:13875-13878）：(dx,dy) 旋转 -π/2×player.direction×gravDir

284	            add(new Arrow(a.x, a.y, dy / d * s, -dx / d * s, starDmg, this.knockback, 1039, null,
285	              { grav: 0, life: 240 }), 'projectiles');
286	          }

964	 *  （每弹一只）。本仓单玩家:debuff 145 已由 MoonLeechProj 贴身授予 → 玩家身中
965	 *  MoonLeech 即召,等价替换旧「弹已射出即命中」无条件近似
966	 *  (ai[1] 源弹索引以 -1 代,moonLeechAI 以出生点代弹着点,不受影响)。 */
967	function spawnMoonLeech(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, player: Player): void {
968	  if (!player.buffs.moonLeech) return;
969	  const leech = spawnPart(game, head, 401);
970	  if (!leech) return;
971	  leech.x = player.cx - leech.w / 2;
972	  leech.y = player.cy - leech.h / 2;
973	  leech.ai0 = head.id + 1;
974	  leech.ai1 = -1;
975	  leech.ai2 = 0;
976	  leech.vx = 0; leech.vy = 0;
977	}
978	
979	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

321	  reel(game: GameHooks): number {
322	    if (this.biteT <= 0 || (!this.catchId && !this.catchEnemy)) return 0;
323	    const p = this.ownerRef();
324	    if (!p) return 0;
325	    // 声呐药水（buff 122）：咬钩时已掷——由 Game 侧显示（本实体的 catchId 预存）
326	    this.consumeBait(p);
327	    const caught = this.catchId;
328	    this.catchId = 0;
329	    this.dead = true;
330	    void game;
331	    return caught;
332	  }
333	
334	  /** 鱼饵消耗（:51640：1/(1+bait/6)，钓具箱 +1 分母） */
335	  private consumeBait(p: Player): boolean {
336	    const slot = p.inv.slots[this.baitSlot];
337	    if (!slot) return false;
338	    const chance = 1 / (1 + this.baitPower / 6 + (this.tackleBox(p) ? 1 : 0));
339	    if (Math.random() < chance) p.inv.removeAt(this.baitSlot, 1);
340	    return true;
341	  }

339	    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）；
340	    // 炮弹族：162 penetrate4 / 281 penetrate-1（SetDefaults）→ 穿透，撞块/超时才 Kill 爆炸；
341	    // 166 雪球 penetrate1 → 一碰即碎
342	    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame'
343	      || this.kind === 'cannonball' || this.kind === 'bunny';
344	    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
345	      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;
346	    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）。
347	    // 反射后的弹体 friendly 已翻——不再入敌怪判定（原版 Damage NPC 门禁）
348	    if (!this.reflected) {
349	      const trapProjId = TRAP_PROJ_ID[this.kind];
350	      for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
351	        const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] }; reflectsProjectiles?: boolean };
352	        if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;
353	        // 反射源命中（Projectile.cs:12050-12060）：友方炮弹/雪球可反射（aiStyle2）
354	        if (trapProjId !== undefined && tryReflectProjectile(this, trapProjId, e, game)) return;
355	        if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
356	          playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
357	          game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
358	        }
359	        if (!pierce) {
360	          if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);
361	          this.dead = true;
362	          return;
363	        }
364	      }
365	    }
366	    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）到此为止：不误伤小动物/城镇 NPC
367	    // （原版 friendly 弹对城镇 NPC 免疫；小动物不在敌怪判定内）。
368	    // 反射后转 hostile 同样走此出口（原版敌弹对 NPC 无伤害）
369	    if (!this.hostile || this.reflected) return;
370	    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）
371	    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;
372	    if (buckets) {
373	      for (const c of buckets.critters) {
374	        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;
375	        c.hurt(game);
376	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
377	      }
378	      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）
379	      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {
380	        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;
381	        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
382	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
383	      }
384	    }

Bezoar 887 / Adhesive Bandage 885 / Vitamins 892 / Armor Bracing 901 / Medicated Bandage 902 / Ankh Charm 1612 / Obsidian Skull 193 have NO accfx entries at all, and more importantly those item IDs are NOT defined in data/items.ts (only 437 items; grep finds only cobalt_shield 156, lava_charm 906, diving gear 394, arctic 1861 among that family) → the items don't even exist in-game, so no immunity can ever be equipped.

### Q3: WEAPON→ENEMY DEBUFF APPLICATION

Four chains:
(a) Melee swing: Game.ts:7613-7621 (imbue table) — Venom 5-10s, Cursed 3-7s, OnFire 3-7s, Midas 2s (note: assignment not max! `e.midasT = 2` — could shorten; deviation), Ichor 10-20s, Confused 1-4s, Poison 5-10s, Party confetti.
(b) Melee-projectile family (flail/yoyo/spear/boomerang via WeaponProj): WeaponProj.ts:102 applyProjStatus + :105-110 applyMeleeImbue (projStatus.ts:50-67 same durations).
(c) Whips: WhipProj.ts:246 applyProjStatus + :248 applyMeleeImbue, plus whip-specific hardcoded debuffs at :291-293 (913 Firecracker/912 Coolwhip → onFireT 4s; 914 Thorn Whip → 20% poisonT 3s).
(d) Ranged/other projectiles (Arrow): Arrow.ts:229-231 (ignite flag → onFireT 5s, set by MinionProj.ts:988 for Firecracker-flame minions... wait, that's in MinionProj for flame whip minions) + Arrow.ts:234 applyProjStatus.

projStatus table: projStatus.ts:17-25 SUPPORTED maps only 7 vanilla buff ids → enemy fields:
24→onFireT, 20→poisonT, 70→venomT, 39→cursedT, 31→confusedT, 69→ichorT, 72→midasT.
The extracted table (data/vanilla-projstatus.json) contains buff ids: 24(×24), 20(×14), 323(×9), 39(×7), 70(×6), 31(×6), 69(×5), 324(×5), 153(×5), 30(×3), 189(×3), 44(×2), 375(×2), 137(×2), 72(×1), 36(×1), 203(×1), 151(×1). Unsupported ones are silently skipped at projStatus.ts:38 (`if (!field) continue;`).

Inflictable in practice: OnFire, Poisoned, Venom, Cursed Inferno, Confused, Ichor, Midas + Inferno potion aura (Game.ts:6833-6845 → cursedT 2s, mapped to buff 323 OnFire3 but stored in cursedT!) + BOC accessory confusion (Player.ts:1513).

NOT inflictable (declared gap, projStatus.ts:12-16): Frostburn(44), BetsysCurse(203), Bleeding(30), SoulDrain(151), Shadowflame(153), BoneJavelin, StardustBleed, Daybreak(189), Oiled, TentacleSpike, BloodButcherer(344), Hemorrhage(375), BrokenArmor(36), Slimed(137), OnFire3(323)/Frostburn2(324) have no distinct fields. Stoned, Electrified, Omen Sight: not present anywhere.
Note also: Fireball/Frostburn arrows etc: the table has 323 (9 entries) and 324 (5) but those map to nothing → skipped.

DoT rates: Enemy.ts:718-727 onFire 4 HP/s; :736-749 poison 6, venom 30, cursed 24 HP/s; ichor → +7 pen Enemy.ts:4191; midas → coin multiplier Enemy.ts:4354; confused → seekDirX reversal Enemy.ts:380-396 and bossAI_martian.ts:768.

### Q4: NPC buffImmune

- NO vanilla-style NPC.buffImmune table. No undead poison/venom immunity, no fire-immune-on-debuff gating.
- Every inflict writes directly to the field with no immunity check: Game.ts:7613-7621, projStatus.ts:31-44, WeaponProj.ts:102/107, WhipProj.ts:246/248/292-293, Arrow.ts:231/234, Player.ts:1513 (BOC), Game.ts:6841 (Inferno).
- The only NPC immunity gates that exist, both unrelated to weapon debuffs:
  - NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE (data/npcLavaImmune.ts:4-9) gates lava contact damage + self-inflicted OnFire at Enemy.ts:682-698.
  - isNpcShimmerImmune (stats/Shimmer.ts:304-308) gates shimmer conversion at Enemy.ts:709.
- Water clears enemy OnFire: Enemy.ts:700.
- Dead data: `fireImmune?: boolean` declared in data/enemies.ts:23 and set true on two hell enemies (data/enemies.ts:79, 86) but NEVER read anywhere — a grep for `fireImmune` yields only those three lines. So enemies flagged fireImmune still take onFire from weapons/lava etc. (Well — they'd be in NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE for lava, but weapon-applied OnFire is not gated.)
- An inflict on a would-be-immune NPC simply succeeds (no check, no log).

### Q5: AddBuff merge semantics

- Buffs.ts:276-279: `apply` sets only if undefined or current < durationSec → max merge, no shortening. Confirmed.
- `set` (Buffs.ts:288-290) is the raw assignment used for shimmer fall-through (Player.ts:809).
- Campfire: Game.ts:2697-2715, scan every 20 ticks (`this.tickCount % 20 === 0`), apply(Campfire, 0.5) at :2713 — 0.5 s refresh vs 1/3 s scan interval → stays up while in range. Heart Lantern is NOT a buff: `buffs.lanternHeal = heart ? 1 : 0` at :2714, consumed in BuffState.tick (Buffs.ts:367-373) as 1 HP/s.
- Aura four: Game.ts:2721-2743, every 20 ticks, apply(...,0.5) for StarInBottle(:2738)/Sunflower(:2739, gated by scene.hasSunflower)/CatBast(:2740)/PeaceCandle(:2741).
- Honey: Player.ts:778, every tick while immersed, apply(Honey, 30) → constant 30 s while in honey, natural countdown after leaving.
- Other re-appliers: Sharpened station proximity Player.ts:1502 (every 60 ticks, 2 s), Werewolf Player.ts:1489 (every tick, 2 s), Shimmered Player.ts:783/796 (1 s) — all rely on max-merge.

# Buff / Debuff System Audit — `game/src`

**Caveat:** `Player.ts` and `Game.ts` were modified *while* I was auditing (line numbers drifted +3 twice). Every line number below was re-verified against the current files at the end of the session.

---

## 1. PLAYER-SIDE DEBUFF SOURCES (everything that can be RECEIVED)

`BuffType` is declared at `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/stats/Buffs.ts:19-124`. There are only **12 player-facing debuff-ish entries** in the entire enum: `OnFire(7)`, `Burning(8)`, `Bleeding(9)`, `Suffocation(10)`, `Chilled(62)`, `Frozen(63)`, `ManaSickness(69)`, `MoonLeech(67)`, `Obstructed(68)`, `Weak(73)`, `Shimmered(77)`, `Stinky(76)`.

### Every grant site (complete list — `buffs.apply` has exactly 27 call sites repo-wide)

| Buff | Source | File:line |
|---|---|---|
| `OnFire` 7s | Lava immersion (gated by `obsidianSkin`) | `entities/Player.ts:843-851` |
| `Burning` 0.33s (refreshed) | Hot-tile contact — meteorite/hellstone family (`TOUCH_HOT`) | `entities/Player.ts:1922`, table `stats/TouchDamage.ts:31` |
| `Bleeding` 10–20s | Spike family (`TOUCH_BLEEDING`) | `entities/Player.ts:1923`, `stats/TouchDamage.ts:29` |
| `Suffocation` 1 tick | Sand burial (`SUFFOCATE`) | `entities/Player.ts:1927`, `stats/TouchDamage.ts:33` |
| `Chilled` 2.5s | Expert-mode snow-biome cold water (gated by `arcticDivingGear`) | `entities/Player.ts:871` |
| `Chilled` 5/10s + `Frozen` 1/1.5/2.5% chance | Hostile projectile **348 FrostWave** | `entities/projTargets.ts:204-210` |
| `Chilled` 5s (1/3) or 2.5s (else 1/2) | Hostile projectile **349 FrostShard** | `entities/projTargets.ts:212-215` |
| `Weak` 30s | **NPC contact damage from Werewolf enemy (vanillaId 155)** — the ONLY enemy-contact debuff | `core/Game.ts:9026` (inside `damagePlayer`, `core/Game.ts:9019` comment) |
| `MoonLeech` 14s | Moon Lord leech projectile 456 within 20px | `entities/bossAI_duke_moonlord.ts:939` |
| `Obstructed` ~1s | Nebula Headcrab 421 head-attach, re-applied every tick | `entities/bossAI_lunar_misc.ts:1275` |
| `Shimmered` 1s / 0.1s `set` | Shimmer immersion / fall-through | `entities/Player.ts:783, 796, 809` |
| `ManaSickness` 5s | Drinking mana potion (manual + Mana Flower auto) | `core/Game.ts:3453`, `core/Game.ts:4080` |
| `Resistance` (=PotionSickness) 60s | Drinking heal potion | `core/Game.ts:4076` |

### NPC contact damage — the answer to your specific question
`entities/Enemy.ts:758-767` is the contact branch. It does **two** things only:
- Honey Slime (NPC 1, `ai1===1125`) → grants **Honey buff** (`Enemy.ts:763`), no damage.
- Otherwise `game.damagePlayer(...)` (`Enemy.ts:766`), which flows to `core/Game.ts:9026` — **Werewolf (155) → Weak. That is the entire NPC-contact debuff catalogue.**

### What is DEAD (no enum member, no grant, no effect)
`Poisoned`, `Venom`, `Cursed Inferno`, `Ichor`, `Frostburn`, `Darkness`, `Silenced`, `Slow`, `BrokenArmor`, `Stunned`, `Electrified`, `Blackout` (as a buff), `BetsysCurse`, `Stoned`, `Oiled`, `Shadowflame`, `SoulDrain`, `Daybreak`, `Invisibility`, `Warmth` — **none of these exist as `BuffType` members at all.** Verified: the only enum names matching any debuff family are the four listed in the table above plus `Bleeding`/`Chilled`/`Frozen`/`MoonLeech`.

Two extra gaps worth flagging:
- `statusPlayer` (`entities/projTargets.ts:201-220`) is a `switch` whose `default` is a no-op; only 348/349 are wired. The file's own comment at `projTargets.ts:200` admits "其余弹型的 debuff 链(毒/诅咒/灵液/暗影焰等)随对应批次逐条接入" — so Hornet stings, Cursed Hammer, Ichor Sticker, Paladin, etc. deal damage but apply **nothing**.
- `entities/Dart.ts` calls `hitPlayer` at `Dart.ts:331, 784, 802, 814` but **never calls `statusPlayer`** → trap/boss darts can never debuff the player even if their projId were wired. `MagicProj.ts` / `MinionProj.ts` / `TownShot.ts` have no player-hit status path either.
- Torch God "Blackout" is **not** a buff — it's the `Game.blackout` boolean (`core/Game.ts:499-500`, set from `world/TorchGod.ts:68, 77, 124`), consumed at `core/Game.ts:3158` / `lighting/lightTables.ts:35-40`.

## 2. PLAYER `buffImmune` / IMMUNITY — **does not exist**

- There is **no** `buffImmune` array/set anywhere in `game/src`. Grepping `buffImmune|fireImmune|lavaImmune|poisonImmune|debuffImmune` yields only comment prose.
- **No accessory grants debuff immunity.** The `AccFx` interface (`data/vanillaAccFx.ts:7-78`) has no immunity field of any kind. `data/vanilla-accfx.json` has no poison/venom/cursed/ichor key.
- The vanilla Ankh family is simply absent as items: `Bezoar(887)`, `AdhesiveBandage(885)`, `Vitamins(892)`, `ArmorBracing(901)`, `MedicatedBandage(902)`, `AnkhCharm(1612)`, `ObsidianSkull(193)` have **no entry in `vanilla-accfx.json` and are not defined in `data/items.ts`** (only 437 items; grep for those ids only matches `i18n/idNames.generated.ts`). They cannot be obtained, let alone grant immunity.
- `AnkhShield(1613)` *is* in the accfx JSON but only as `{fireWalk:1, noKnockback:1}` (`vanilla-accfx.json:639-640`) — and item 1613 is also **not in `items.ts`**, so even that is unreachable.

The only immunity surfaces that do exist:
| Surface | What it blocks | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `ObsidianSkin` buff | Lava damage + lava `OnFire` + hot tiles | `entities/Player.ts:843` (`!this.buffs.obsidianSkin` gate), `:891` (fireWalk), getter `stats/Buffs.ts:491-493` |
| `equipStats.fireWalk` (Obsidian Skull family / Molten set) | Hot-tile `Burning` only | `stats/TouchDamage.ts:44-48`, `entities/Player.ts:891`; sources `data/vanilla-accfx.json:62(193),162(396),166(397),316(907),321(908),923(4003),942(4038),1001(5000)`, `data/vanillaArmorSets.ts:62` |
| `equipStats.arcticDivingGear` (1861) | Cold-water `Chilled` in expert snow | `entities/Player.ts:871` gate |
| `equipStats.lavaMax` (Lava Charm 906 etc.) | Lava grace ticks, not a debuff immunity | `entities/Player.ts:843-860` |
| Water | Clears `OnFire` | `entities/Player.ts:865` |
| Honey | Offsets DoT (not immunity) | `stats/Buffs.ts:353-356` |

Vanilla's `environmentBuffImmunityTimer` (Ankh Shield / Cross Necklace family) is explicitly marked unimplemented at the comment above `entities/Player.ts:871` ("environmentBuffImmunityTimer（十字盾族 :27692 第 6 门）本作未实装 → 恒 0 语义").

## 3. WEAPON → ENEMY DEBUFF APPLICATION

Enemy-side state lives as **plain numeric fields, not a buff table**: `onFireT` `Enemy.ts:354`, `poisonT` `Enemy.ts:362`, `venomT` `Enemy.ts:363`, `cursedT` `Enemy.ts:364`, `ichorT` `Enemy.ts:369`, `midasT` `Enemy.ts:371`, `confusedT` `Enemy.ts:373`. Tick-out in `Enemy.fixedUpdate` at `Enemy.ts:718-727` (onFire 4 HP/s) and `Enemy.ts:736-754` (poison 6 / venom 30 / cursed 24 HP/s, ichor/midas/confused countdown).

### Four infliction chains
1. **Melee swing** — `core/Game.ts:7612-7622` (inline imbue table, inside the swing hit loop starting `Game.ts:7589`):
   `ImbueVenom`→venomT 5–10s (`:7613`), `ImbueCursed`→cursedT 3–7s (`:7614`), `ImbueFire`→onFireT 3–7s (`:7615`), `ImbueGold`→**`e.midasT = 2` (`:7616`, plain assignment, can shorten — inconsistent with AddBuff max semantics)**, `ImbueIchor`→ichorT 10–20s (`:7617`), `ImbueNano`→confusedT 1–4s (`:7618`), `ImbuePoison`→poisonT 5–10s (`:7619`), `ImbueParty`→confetti particles (`:7620-7622`).
2. **Melee-projectile family (flail / yoyo / spear / boomerang)** — `entities/WeaponProj.ts:102` (`applyProjStatus`) + `WeaponProj.ts:105-110` (`applyMeleeImbue`, only when `meleeProj`), implementation `entities/projStatus.ts:50-67` (same durations as above, all max-merged).
3. **Whips** — `entities/WhipProj.ts:246` (`applyProjStatus`) + `WhipProj.ts:248` (`applyMeleeImbue`, unconditional for whips), plus whip-specific hardcoded debuffs `WhipProj.ts:291-293` (proj 913 Firecracker / 912 Cool Whip → `onFireT` 4s standing in for On Fire! 3; proj 914 Thorn Whip → 20% `poisonT` 3s).
4. **Arrows / generic projectiles** — `entities/Arrow.ts:229-231` (`ignite` flag → `onFireT` 5s; the flag is set at `entities/MinionProj.ts:988` for whip-summoned flames) + `Arrow.ts:234` (`applyProjStatus`).

`applyProjStatus` (`projStatus.ts:31-44`) rolls against the extracted vanilla table `data/vanilla-projstatus.json`, maps via `SUPPORTED` (`projStatus.ts:17-25`) = **{24→onFireT, 20→poisonT, 70→venomT, 39→cursedT, 31→confusedT, 69→ichorT, 72→midasT}**, and silently skips anything else (`projStatus.ts:38`).

### Actually inflictable vs. declared-but-missing
**Inflictable:** On Fire, Poisoned, Venom, Cursed Inferno, Confused, Ichor, Midas — plus two non-table sources: the **Inferno potion aura** (`core/Game.ts:6833-6845`, sets `en.cursedT = 2` at `:6841` while the comment claims buff 323 On Fire! 3 — stored in the wrong field) and the **Brain of Confusion accessory** confusion pulse (`entities/Player.ts:1509-1513`).

**Not inflictable.** The JSON table contains these ids with no backing field, so they are dropped at `projStatus.ts:38`: `323` OnFire3 (×9 entries), `324` Frostburn2 (×5), `153` Shadowflame (×5), `30` Bleeding (×3), `189` Daybreak (×3), `44` Frostburn (×2), `375` Hemorrhage (×2), `137` Slimed (×2), `36` BrokenArmor (×1), `203` BetsysCurse (×1), `151` SoulDrain (×1). The full GAP list is documented at `projStatus.ts:12-16`. **`Stoned`, `Electrified`, `Omen Sight`, `Betsys Curse` (as armor-break), `Oiled` do not exist anywhere in the repo.**

Downstream consumption of enemy debuffs: ichor → +7 armor pen `Enemy.ts:4191`; midas → coin drop multiplier `Enemy.ts:4354`; confused → `seekDirX` direction reversal `Enemy.ts:380-396`, consumed by AIs (e.g. `bossAI_martian.ts:768`).

## 4. NPC-SIDE `buffImmune` — **does not exist**

- There is **no** vanilla-`NPC.buffImmune` equivalent. No undead poison/venom resistance, no fire-immune-enemy debuff gating. Every inflict writes straight to the field with **zero immunity check**: `Game.ts:7613-7621`, `projStatus.ts:31-44`, `WeaponProj.ts:102/107`, `WhipProj.ts:246/248/292-293`, `Arrow.ts:231/234`, `Player.ts:1513`, `Game.ts:6841`.
- When an inflict is attempted on a "would-be-immune" NPC, **it simply succeeds** — no check, no early return, no log.
- The only NPC immunity gates present, both orthogonal to weapon debuffs:
  - `NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE` (`data/npcLavaImmune.ts:4-9`) — gates lava contact damage *and the self-inflicted lava OnFire* at `Enemy.ts:682-698` (apply at `:689`).
  - `isNpcShimmerImmune` (`stats/Shimmer.ts:304-308`) — gates shimmer conversion at `Enemy.ts:709`.
- Water clears enemy `OnFire` at `Enemy.ts:700` (`if (this.inWater && !this.lavaWet) this.onFireT = 0;`) — the closest thing to a conditional cleanse.
- **Dead data:** `fireImmune?: boolean` is declared at `data/enemies.ts:23` and set on two hell enemies (`data/enemies.ts:79` Lava Slime, `:86` Hellbat) but is **never read anywhere** (grep for `fireImmune` returns exactly those three lines). Those enemies still take weapon-applied `OnFire`.

---

## 5. `AddBuff` MERGE SEMANTICS + CAMPFIRE / LANTERN / HONEY LOOPS

**`apply` is max-merge, confirmed** — `stats/Buffs.ts:276-279`:
```ts
apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
  const cur = this.active.get(type);
  if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
}
```
Never shortens. (`set` at `Buffs.ts:288-290` is the raw-assignment escape hatch, used only for shimmer fall-through at `Player.ts:809`.)

Refresh loops and their durations:
| Loop | Cadence | Duration | File:line |
|---|---|---|---|
| Campfire | every 20 ticks | **0.5 s** | `core/Game.ts:2697` (gate), `:2713` (`apply(BuffType.Campfire, 0.5)`) — 20t = 1/3 s < 0.5 s so it never lapses while in range |
| Heart Lantern | every 20 ticks | **not a buff** — writes `buffs.lanternHeal = heart ? 1 : 0` | `core/Game.ts:2714`; consumed in `Buffs.ts:367-373` as 1 HP/s |
| Star in a Bottle / Sunflower / Cat Bast / Peace Candle | every 20 ticks | 0.5 s each | `core/Game.ts:2721` (gate), `:2738`, `:2739` (gated by `scene.hasSunflower`), `:2740`, `:2741` |
| Honey immersion | **every tick** | **30 s** | `entities/Player.ts:778` → constant 30 s while immersed, natural countdown after leaving |
| Shimmer | every tick | 1 s | `entities/Player.ts:783, 796` |
| Werewolf (Moon Charm) | every tick | 2 s | `entities/Player.ts:1489` |
| Sharpening Station proximity | every 60 ticks | 2 s | `entities/Player.ts:1502` |
| Brain of Confusion pulse | event-driven | 4 s | `entities/Player.ts:1509` |

All of these depend on max-merge; the file header documents the invariant at `Buffs.ts:2-4`.

---

## 6. WHERE `BuffState` GETTERS REACH GAME MATH

**The aggregation hub is `entities/Player.ts`** — the `equipStats` builder (~`Player.ts:218-512`) plus stat getters `Player.ts:513-654` and the per-frame update `Player.ts:662+`. `BuffState` getters themselves are `stats/Buffs.ts:398-523`.

### Wired into math (verified consumers)
- `defenseBonus` → `Player.defense` `Player.ts:213-214`
- `moveSpeedMult` → `Player.ts:993`
- `healthBonus` / `healBlocked` → `Player.ts:519`; heal block gate `Game.ts:3977`, `Game.ts:4062`
- Lifeforce → `Player.maxHp` `Player.ts:518`
- `manaMaxBonus` (Clairvoyance) → `Player.maxMana` `Player.ts:522`
- `maxMinions` (Bewitched + **Summoning**) → `Player.ts:530-533`; `maxSentries` (**WarTable**) → `Player.ts:536-538`
- `hasThorns` → `Player.ts:540-541` → reflect at `Game.ts:9101`
- `attackSpeedMult` (Tipsy/Werewolf/Weak/wellFedMeleeSpeed) → `Player.ts:548-556`
- `damageMult` (Rage/BOC/MagicPower/manaSick/Archery/Tipsy/Werewolf/Weak/wellFedDamage) → `Player.ts:559-588`; arrow split `Player.ts:600`
- `meleeArmorPen` (**Sharpened +12 — this IS wired**, see below) → `Player.ts:623-625` → used `Game.ts:4208, 7583, 7602`
- `critChance` (Wrath/BOC/Tipsy/Werewolf/wellFedCrit) → `Player.ts:640-654`
- `buffs.tick` → `Player.ts:672`; `consumeDotChunk` → `Player.ts:679`
- natural-regen block (Honey/Bleeding/**Obstructed**) → `Player.ts:700-703`
- mana regen (**StarInBottle** ×2, ManaRegen) → `Player.ts:724-745`
- `canWaterWalk` → `Player.ts:770`; `obsidianSkin` → `Player.ts:843, 891`; `gills` → `Player.ts:920`
- **Frozen** input lock → `Player.ts:1002`; FlipperPotion swim → `Player.ts:1117`
- Titan melee KB ×1.5 → `Player.ts:1264`, `Game.ts:7609`
- `slowFall` → `Player.ts:1332, 1341`; Heartreach → `Player.ts:1784`
- Luck / **Stinky** / `kiteLuckLevel` → `Player.ts:1813-1828` (with `stats/Luck.ts`); Endurance → `Player.ts:1854`
- `arrowSpeedMult` (Archery) → `Game.ts:4122`; **AmmoReservation** 20% save → `Game.ts:4164`
- Mining ×0.75 → `Game.ts:4884`; `pickSpeedMult` (wellFed + KiteBuff) → `Game.ts:4887`
- Builder range +1 → `Game.ts:1192, 3596, 3912, 5574, 5639, 5943`; wall speed → `Game.ts:5603, 5612`
- NightOwl → light decay `Game.ts:3158`; Shine → light `Game.ts:3234`; Inferno light `Game.ts:3235`
- Gravitation → `Game.ts:2335`
- **Inferno aura** burn → `Game.ts:6833-6845`
- **Battle / Calming / Sunflower / PeaceCandle spawn rate** → passed at `Game.ts:7719`, applied in `world/spawn/VanillaSpawner.ts:727-735` (Calming ×1.65/×0.6, Sunflower ×1.2/×0.8), `:737-740` (Battle ×0.5/×2), `:746-750` (PeaceCandle ×1.3/×0.7)
- Fishing / Tipsy(fishing) → `entities/Bobber.ts:107-108`; Crate → `Bobber.ts:221`
- Spelunker / Dangersense / **BiomeSight** highlight overlay → `render/Renderer.ts:4011-4037` (`drawBuffHighlights`, declared `Renderer.ts:4005-4009`)
- **MoonLeech** → lifesteal block `Game.ts:9199`; Moon Leech spawn gate `bossAI_duke_moonlord.ts:968`
- **Obstructed** → regen block `Player.ts:701, 703`; screen obstruction `render/Renderer.ts:3787`

### Declared-but-unused / dead (audit findings)
1. **`hasAmmoBox` getter (`Buffs.ts:425-427`) has zero consumers.** The Ammo Box buff is granted (`Game.ts:6063`) and shows an icon, but its 20% ammo-save is implemented only for `AmmoReservation` (`Game.ts:4164`). Buff is mechanically inert.
2. **`Hunter` (buff 17) has no consumer at all** — only its `BUFF_DEFS` entry (`Buffs.ts:189`) and a *mention* in the `drawBuffHighlights` doc comment (`Renderer.ts:4005`, which only tests spel/dang/biomeS at `:4014`). `detectCreature` was never ported.
3. **`Sonar` (122) is granted but never read.** `Bobber.sonarLabel()` (`Bobber.ts:355-361`) returns the catch name unconditionally and itself has **zero callers** — the sonar display is dead code, buff or no buff.
4. **`KiteBuff` (383)** — all three getters are consumed (`Buffs.ts:406, 464, 456` → `Player.ts:1828`) but there is **no grant source** (kite items not ported; `Buffs.ts:105-107` says so). Pure dead weight.
5. **`Stinky` (120)** — consumed at `Player.ts:1813` but never granted (Stink Potion 1332 / projectile 1080 not ported; `Buffs.ts:108-110`).
6. **Stale comment:** `Buffs.ts:46` still says Sharpened armor-pen is "穿甲系统未接先登记" ("registered, system not wired") — **it IS wired** at `Player.ts:623-625` → `Game.ts:4208/7583/7602`. Same class of stale note at `Buffs.ts:48` ("召唤位未接先登记" for Bewitched) and `Player.ts:532` ("War Table buff 348 … 未实装暂缺") — both are in fact implemented at `Player.ts:530-538`.

### Separate channel worth knowing about
Whip tag buffs (308/311/312/314/365) are **not** in `BuffState` — they live in `Player.whipBuffs: Record<number, number>` (`Player.ts:137`), ticked at `Player.ts:1581-1582`, granted at `WhipProj.ts:297-298` (tables `entities/WhipTag.ts:27-32`), consumed at `Game.ts:4541-4542` (whip tag damage), `WhipProj.ts:109-110`, `MinionProj.ts:995` (Durendal/Kaleidoscope sentinel gating).
````
